./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g4-path-version.txt:137:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:371:| Decision | Versioned/current catalog、stream和export request必须显式、无default地选择`read_binding = EXACT(binding) | LATEST_AT_START`。Versioned EXACT携带CommitId，LATEST_AT_START携带RefName并在真正打开provider snapshot时resolve；Current EXACT携带CurrentTableGenerationId，LATEST_AT_START在打开SQLite read transaction时取得current generation。所有response、stream header、cursor和artifact manifest必须返回并绑定实际resolved CommitId/generation；一旦resolved，同一次读取保持pinned snapshot。Async export的LATEST_AT_START指worker开始获取snapshot时，不是request acceptance时；多个独立LATEST请求不承诺同一版本。DiffRefs等天然比较两个明确历史点的operation继续要求exact commits。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/p2-r6-s8-step-5-old-reference-raw/g4-path-version.txt:138:./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:372:| Rationale | Exact binding为需要可复现结果的caller保留最高保证；LATEST_AT_START让只关心执行时最新数据的caller避免额外resolve/stale-retry，同时不牺牲单次流内部一致性。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:371:| Decision | Versioned/current catalog、stream和export request必须显式、无default地选择`read_binding = EXACT(binding) | LATEST_AT_START`。Versioned EXACT携带CommitId，LATEST_AT_START携带RefName并在真正打开provider snapshot时resolve；Current EXACT携带CurrentTableGenerationId，LATEST_AT_START在打开SQLite read transaction时取得current generation。所有response、stream header、cursor和artifact manifest必须返回并绑定实际resolved CommitId/generation；一旦resolved，同一次读取保持pinned snapshot。Async export的LATEST_AT_START指worker开始获取snapshot时，不是request acceptance时；多个独立LATEST请求不承诺同一版本。DiffRefs等天然比较两个明确历史点的operation继续要求exact commits。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-4-to-7.md:372:| Rationale | Exact binding为需要可复现结果的caller保留最高保证；LATEST_AT_START让只关心执行时最新数据的caller避免额外resolve/stale-retry，同时不牺牲单次流内部一致性。 |

./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:401:| Decision | `P2-PR-CONTRACT` required stream matrix同时覆盖`EXACT`与`LATEST_AT_START`，验证返回resolved binding且stream打开后保持pinned，随后并发write不得改变该结果。Harness使用logical barrier及controllable slow sink，直接证明bounded queue达到配置上限后暂停upstream read；不以固定sleep、RSS猜测或numeric throughput作为correctness oracle。Disconnect、consumer cancel、idle timeout和total deadline必须关闭Dolt reader/SQLite read transaction并产生cleanup evidence；Current long read还必须在既有duration/bytes/batch limit下不无限阻塞WAL/checkpoint。Partial frame、缺失`STREAM_END`、length/hash corruption和oversized frame不得成为成功。Cursor resume只在相同resolved snapshot/query/options及有效codec/TTL下成功；expired、generation变化或unsupported codec返回typed stale/expired failure。Async export在client断连后继续，只有Parquet完整close并通过hash/size/schema/manifest验证才发布ArtifactRef；unpublished staging在success、failure、cancel和restart路径均须证明cleanup或保留为合法recovery root。 |
./docs/backend-vnext/refactoring/temporary/phase-2/decision-ledger-round-8-to-11.md:763:| Decision | v1 artifact ingress使用one-shot authenticated streaming upload。Caller提交idempotency key、media type、declared size/raw digest、content contract和intended action；Service持久化最小repository-bound upload/staging record，完整close、measured validation及manifest publication后返回ArtifactRef。中途disconnect/restart不发布，caller以原key完整重传；publish后响应丢失时same-key/same-digest返回原ArtifactRef，different digest冲突。v1不提供public multipart/session/chunk、parallel或range-resume upload，也不提供anonymous URL；artifact download使用bounded authenticated stream且不承诺HTTP Range，失败后重新下载。 |
